Title: The Mystery hid from Ages and Generations, made manifest by the Gospel-Revelation: or, the Salvation of All Men the grand thing aimed at in the scheme of God, as opened in the New-Testament writings, and entrusted with Jesus Christ to bring into effect
Creator: Charles Chauncy (the title page in the scan names no author)
Publisher: London: printed for Charles Dilly, in the Poultry
Publication Date: 1784
Rights: Public Domain (published 1784, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT')
Source: Internet Archive item mysteryhidfromag00chau (https://archive.org/details/mysteryhidfromag00chau), the item's own mysteryhidfromag00chau_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Princeton Theological Seminary Library; host metadata date: 1784. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 37929cf449d0c785f04c199fac8cb3226173ca74344f75a5e14966b454e4e308.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Princeton Theological Seminary copy from the Universalist Historical Library of the Crane Theological School, Tufts; good OCR with the long s as f. The title page names no author; the work is Chauncy's by the host catalogue and by the 1790 reply of Jonathan Edwards (vendored here), which names him. A Dilly booklist closes the volume.
Content note: The Boston minister's long-withheld case for universal salvation: a paraphrase and commentary on Romans 5 and the other Pauline texts, arguing that all men will finally be saved after a remedial punishment; published in London without the author's name.
